# IBC contract

The [IBC modules](../../../interchain/modules) implement clients, connections, channels, transfer and interchain accounts. The node exposes their messages, queries, CLI commands and precompile integration through the surfaces below.

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | IBC core GenesisState field 4 params (interchain/proto/ibc/core/types/v1/genesis.proto) with inbound_enabled and outbound_enabled |
| storage | The IBC param subspace keys InboundEnabled and OutboundEnabled (interchain/modules/core/types/params.go) |
| errors | Validate errors of Params wrapped into genesis validation |
| events | None |
| gas | Genesis only |
| invariants | Exported flags equal the live param subspace values |
| pause | The flags themselves are the inbound and outbound pause |

| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-param-receive-enabled</code> | <code>genesis_param</code> | [interchain/modules/apps/transfer/types/transfer.pb.go](../../../interchain/modules/apps/transfer/types/transfer.pb.go) | <code>Params.receive_enabled (param key KeyReceiveEnabled)</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | IBC core GenesisState field 4 params (interchain/proto/ibc/core/types/v1/genesis.proto) with inbound_enabled and outbound_enabled |
| storage | The IBC param subspace keys InboundEnabled and OutboundEnabled (interchain/modules/core/types/params.go) |
| errors | Validate errors of Params wrapped into genesis validation |
| events | None |
| gas | Genesis only |
| invariants | Exported flags equal the live param subspace values |
| pause | The flags themselves are the inbound and outbound pause |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | IBC core GenesisState field 4 params (interchain/proto/ibc/core/types/v1/genesis.proto) with inbound_enabled and outbound_enabled |
| storage | The IBC param subspace keys InboundEnabled and OutboundEnabled (interchain/modules/core/types/params.go) |
| errors | Validate errors of Params wrapped into genesis validation |
| events | None |
| gas | Genesis only |
| invariants | Exported flags equal the live param subspace values |
| pause | The flags themselves are the inbound and outbound pause |
